Let's implement this Lp update directly in code. It doesn't require LP. n=2048. Initialize a = flat coefficients normalized to sum1: a_i = 1/√(i+1), a/=a.sum(). Also initialize other profiles: reversed flat, random smooth, sym, constant, etc.

Update procedure:
```
def lpx_update(a, p, step, active_all=True):
    C = conv_fft(a)  # length 2n-1, sum 1
    C = np.maximum(C, 0)
    # weights
    if p == 1: w = 1/(len(C))
    else:
       logw = (p-1)*np.log(C + eps)
       logw -= logw.max()
       w = exp(logw); w/=w.sum()
    # h_i = Σ w_k a_{k-i}
    h = weighted_conv(a, w)  # Can compute via FFT: h_i = cross-correlation.
```

def optimize_lp_update(a, n, time, p_start=2, p_end=32, steps_eta=[0.5,0.1,...]):
    best=a, best_score=score(a)
    cur=a, cur_score=best
    p=p_start
    eta=0.1
    for it:
      C=conv(cur); score; if score<...
      logw=(p-1)*log(C+eps); w=... normalized
      h=conv_w_rev(cur)
      mean=dot(cur,h)
      c=h/mean -1
      for eta in attempt: a_new=cur*exp(-eta*c); normalize; score; if better accept and refine? else reduce eta.
      p = p_start*(p_end/p_start)**(it/iters) or increase slowly.
```

def optimize_sequence(a0, stop_time, p_schedule, initial_eta=0.1, max_stale=2000):
    a = a0.copy(); a/=a.sum()
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    eta=initial_eta
    stale=0
    while time.monotonic()<stop_time:
       C=conv(a); score=score_from(a,C); if score<best_score: best=a; best_score=score; stale=0 else stale++
       if stale>3000: break or add noise
       p = p_schedule[min(step, len-1)] maybe use iteration count
       c = direction(a,p,C)
       accepted=False
       for try_eta in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1):
           new = a * exp(-try_eta*c); new=np.clip(new,1e-300,None); new/=new.sum()
           newC=conv(new); newS=... newScore=...
           if newScore < best_score: # accept only if improves global/local? Use local cur maybe not best? To avoid oscillation, accept if < cur_score.
              cur_score=newScore; a=new; eta=try_eta*1.1; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           eta*=0.5
           stale+=1
           if eta<1e-4:
              # random restart from best with small noise
              a = best * exp(normal(0,0.05)); a/=sum; cur_score=fast_score(a); eta=0.1; stale=0
           continue
       step_idx += ...
```

Let's implement `smooth_random_noise` efficiently:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=8, scale=1.0):
    t = np.arange(n)/n
    z = np.zeros(n)
    w = 1.0
    for m in range(1, max_freq+1):
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       z += w * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + phase)
       w *= 0.7
    return z * scale
```
Top-level. No lambda.

def unique_candidates(cands, tol=1e-4):
  unique=[]

     is_dupe=False
     for _,b in unique:
        if a.shape==b.shape and np.max(np.abs(a-b)) < tol:

LP update function top-level:
```
def lp_improve(a, stop_time, max_iter=8):
    n=len(a); if n>512: a=downsample? maybe if call with small flat.
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-10); a/=a.sum()
    f=a.copy(); cur=fast_score(f); best=f; best_score=cur
    offsets=list(range(n))
    # Prebuild A once because f changes? A depends on f coefficients, so rebuild each iteration.
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>stop: break
       F,C=fft_a_and_conv(f)
       M=C.max(); S=f.sum()
       if M<=0: break
       data=[np.full(n, f[m], dtype=float) for m in range(n)]
       A=sparse.diags(data, offsets=offsets, shape=(2*n-1,n), format='csc')
       res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=M*np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':'off'})
       if res.status not in (0,3)? status 0 optimal, 1 infeas,2 unbounded,3 iteration limit,4 numerical. If 0/3 use x.
       g=res.x if hasattr
       g=np.maximum(g,0); Sg=g.sum(); if Sg<=0: break
       g_scaled = g * S / Sg
       improved=False
       for tval in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
           f_new=(1-tval)*f + tval*g_scaled
           f_new=np.clip(f_new,1e-10,None); f_new/=f_new.sum()
           sc=fast_score(f_new)
           if sc < cur - 1e-12:
              f=f_new; cur=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved: break
       if sc<best_score: best=f; best_score=sc
    return best,best_score
```
But if no improvement after LP, returns original. For flat, maybe no. Use stop_time.

```
def resample_to_length(a, n_out):
  n=a.size
  if n==n_out: return a.copy()
  xs=np.linspace(0,1,n); xo=np.linspace(0,1,n_out)
  la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
  lo=np.interp(xo, xs, la)
  b=np.exp(np.clip(lo, -50, 50)); return normalize
```

def block_perturb_search(a, stop_time, seed):

  while time<stop:
     width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//500), max(2,n//20)))
     start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
     candidate=best.copy(); candidate[start:start+width]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1))
     # optionally sym average? if rng<0.2: candidate=0.5*(candidate+candidate[::-1]) normalize
     score=fast_score(candidate); if better accept
     # also multiply by global scale? no
```

3. Helpers:
```
def _clip_norm(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a.ndim!=1: a=a.ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-8:
       return None
   return a/s
```
`normalize_positive` return None optional.

```
def get_fft_conv(a, L=None):
   n=a.size
   if L is None: L=next_power_of_two(max(1,2*n-1)) # for score only
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)
   C=C[:2*n-1]
   C[C<0]=0.0
   return F,C
```

6. `fast_score(a)`:

def fast_score(a):
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   if L is None use 2n-1 power
   _,C=get_fft_conv(a)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<1e-12: return inf
   return float(2.0*n*C.max()/(S*S))
```

8. `lpx_direction(a, C, p, F_a, L)`:
```
def lpx_direction(a, C, p, F_a, L):
   n=a.size
   Lc=2*n-1
   C=C.astype(float, copy=False)
   maxC=C.max()
   if maxC <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
   if p <= 1:
       return np.zeros(n)
   pos = C > 1e-300
   logCmax = float(np.log(maxC))
   logC = np.empty(Lc); logC.fill(-1e300); logC[pos]=np.log(C[pos])
   d = (p-1.0)*(logC[pos]-logCmax)
   # Prevent overflow: d should <=0
   d = np.clip(d, -745, 0)
   w_un = np.exp(d)
   q = np.zeros(Lc)
   q[pos]=w_un
   q_sum = float(q.sum())
   if q_sum <= 0 or not finite:
       q = np.full(Lc, 1.0/Lc); A= float(C.sum()/Lc); h=np.ones(n)*(a.sum()/Lc); return h/A-1? but maybe zero.
   q /= q_sum
   logD = (p-1.0)*logCmax + float(np.log(q_sum))
   dnum = p*(logC[pos]-logCmax)
   dnum=np.clip(dnum, -745, 0)
   sum_num = float(np.exp(dnum).sum())
   if sum_num <=0: A=maxC
   else:
       logNum = p*logCmax + float(np.log(sum_num))
       A = float(np.exp(logNum - logD))
       if not finite or A<=0: A=maxC
   # h
   k = np.arange(F_a.size, dtype=np.float64)
   phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * (n-1) * k / L)
   F_ar = phase * np.conjugate(F_a)
   F_q = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
   hfull = np.fft.irfft(F_q * F_ar, L)
   h = hfull[n-1:2*n-1]
   h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
   h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
   c = h / A - 1.0
   c = np.clip(c, -20.0, 20.0)
   return c
```

9. `optimize_sequence(a0, stop_time, seed, global_best=None, global_score=inf, max_p=32)`:
Need no global mutable? We can return best. It can use a global `best_score`? Not necessary.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, stop_time, seed=0, max_p=32):
    a = normalize_positive(a0); if None return None
    L = next_power_of_two(max(1,3*a.size-2))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p_schedule = [2,3,4,6,8,12,16,24,32]
    stage_len = 400
    a = a.copy(); cur_score = fast_score(a); best=a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    eta=0.1; accepted=0; stale=0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
       F,C=get_fft_conv(a,L)
       S=a.sum(); score=2n*C.max()/S^2
       if score < best_score: best_score=score; best=a.copy()
       if score < cur_score - 1e-12: cur_score=score; stale=0 else stale +=1
       if not finite score: break
       if stale > 3000:
          # restart from best
          a = best.copy()
          noise = smooth_noise(a.size, rng, max_freq=6, scale=0.15)
          t = np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
          a = a * np.exp(noise + 0.05*rng.normal()*t)
          a=normalize; eta=0.2; stage=-2000; stale=0; cur_score=fast_score(a); continue
       stage = accepted // stage_len
       p = p_schedule[min(stage, len-1)]
       c = lpx_direction(a,C,p,F_a,L)
       accepted_step=False
       for try_eta in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1, 0.01]:
          new = a * np.exp(-try_eta * c)
          new = np.maximum(new, 1e-300)
          Snew=new.sum(); if not finite or Snew<... reject
          new /= Snew
          Fnew,Cnew=get_fft_conv(new,L)
          new_score=2n*Cnew.max()/new.sum()**2 (sum1)
          if new_score < cur_score - 1e-12:
              a=new; cur_score=new_score; eta=min(0.4, try_eta*1.15); accepted+=1; accepted_step=True; break
       if not accepted_step:
          stale +=1
          eta *=0.7
          if eta < 1e-4:
             # smaller noise in-place
             a = a * np.exp(smooth_noise(..., scale=0.05)); normalize; eta=0.1; cur_score=fast_score(a)
    return best,best_score
```
But in accepted line search, we compute `Fnew,Cnew`; if accepted, we don't use Fnew next loop (it will recompute). Could optimize by storing but fine. For rejected, we computed maybe 4 scores each iteration, expensive. We can limit tries: [eta, eta*0.5, 0.01] maybe. Use only two: eta and eta*0.5; if fail, reduce. Since optimization smooth, okay. To save time, line search at most 2.
```
for try_eta in (eta, eta*0.5): ...
if fail: eta*=0.6; if eta<0.01: noise restart
```
Good.

Need avoid infinite if fast_score of a0 is inf (all zeros). normalize ensures.

`smooth_noise` parameters: `scale` maybe. Use deterministic top-level:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=8, scale=1.0):
   m=max(1,min(int(max_freq), n//2))
   t=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/n
   z=np.zeros(n)
   amp=1.0
   total=0.0
   for ell in range(1,m+1):
      phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*math.pi))
      z += amp * np.cos(2*math.pi*ell*t + phase)
      total += amp
      amp *= 0.6
   if total>0: z *= scale/total
   return z
```

```
def make_static_candidates(n, parent, rng):
   cands=[]
   t = np.arange(n)/float(n)
   # flat
   a = 1/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,n+1)); add
   # reversed flat?
   a = 1/np.sqrt(np.arange(n,0,-1)); add  # reverse may be same objective but different Lp direction? Since initial objective same; optimizing reverse equivalent. skip maybe but add? no.
   # normalizing bit? 
   # powers
   for alpha in [-0.45,-0.3,-0.1,0.0,0.2,0.4]:
       a = (np.arange(1,n+1.0))**alpha; add
   for lam in [-0.5,-0.2,0.2,0.5]:
       base = 1/np.sqrt(np.arange(1,n+1.0))
       a = base * (1.0 + lam * t)
       if min(a)>0: add
   # random profiles 24
   for seed in range(24):
       r=np.random.default_rng(seed)
       x = -0.5*np.log1p(n*t) + r.normal(0,0.2)
       slope = r.uniform(-0.5,1.0)
       x += slope*t
       # random low freq
       x += smooth_noise(n, r, max_freq=5, scale=0.25)
       a=np.exp(np.clip(x, -50, 50)); add
   # parent
   if parent is not None:
       pnp=np.asarray(parent,float); pnp=...; if size>0 and size<=10000: normalize add
   return cands
```
But `np.exp(x)` for x maybe large; normalize handles. Ensure a nonnegative finite.

Random profiles with normal 0.2 at each point may be jagged; maybe okay. Could smooth x by applying `smooth_noise`; random per-point adds jagged. We can keep jagged small.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2 .

The main workhorse is a normalized Lp-smoothing multiplicative optimizer.
For a normalized vector a (sum=1), C=a*a, and a family of smoothed
objectives J_p(C)=(sum C^p)^(1/p), we update
    log a_i  -= eta * ( h_i / A - 1 )
where h is the convolution of a with peak-weighted bins of C and A is the
appropriate weighted mean.  The exponent p is annealed from small values
(flatten the whole convolution) to larger values (focus on the maximum).
The method also uses deterministic fallback profiles, random smooth
restarts, a small LP/SOTA-style refinement, and short block perturbation
polishing.  The best sequence found by fast FFT scoring is returned after a
final exact evaluate_sequence() check.
"""

import math
import time

import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize
import scipy.sparse


TARGET = 1.5027


def normalize_positive(a, floor=1e-300):
    """Normalize a nonnegative 1D array to sum 1; return None if invalid."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    return a


def next_power_of_two(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m, with m>=1."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def score_fft_len(n):
    """FFT length for a*a."""
    return next_power_of_two(max(1, 2 * int(n) - 1))


def opt_fft_len(n):
    """FFT length large enough for both a*a and q*reverse(a)."""
    return next_power_of_two(max(1, 3 * int(n) - 2))


def get_fft_conv(a, fft_len=None):
    """Return (rfft(a), linear a*a) using a zero-padded FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if fft_len is None:
        fft_len = score_fft_len(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    C = np.fft.irfft(F * F, fft_len)
    C = C[:2 * n - 1]
    C = np.where(C > 0.0, C, 0.0)
    return F, C


def score_from_conv(a, C):
    """Exact score from convolution C and vector a."""
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(C.max()) if C.size else float("inf")
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def fast_score(a):
    """Fast FFT score."""
    b = normalize_positive(a)
    if b is None:
        return float("inf")
    _, C = get_fft_conv(b)
    return score_from_conv(b, C)


def lp_norm_from_C(C, p):
    """Stable computation of (sum C**p)^(1/p)."""
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64)
    if C.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    pos = C > 1e-300
    if not np.any(pos):
        return 0.0
    posC = C[pos]
    maxC = float(posC.max())
    if maxC <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(maxC):
        return 0.0
    if p >= 1e8:
        return maxC
    logCmax = float(np.log(maxC))
    d = p * (np.log(posC) - logCmax)
    d = np.clip(d, -745.0, 0.0)
    s = float(np.exp(d).sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return maxC
    val = float(np.exp((math.log(s) + p * logCmax) / p))
    if not math.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0:
        return maxC
    if val > maxC * 1.25:
        return maxC
    return val


def lpx_direction(a, C, p, F_a, fft_len):
    """
    Direction for projected log-update minimizing J_p(C) with sum(a)=1.
    Returns c where a <- a * exp(-eta*c) is a descent step (before clipping).
    """
    n = a.size
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    maxC = float(C.max())
    if maxC <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(maxC):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if p <= 1.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    pos = C > 1e-300
    if not np.any(pos):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    logC = np.empty(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
    logC.fill(-1e300)
    logC[pos] = np.log(C[pos])
    logCmax = float(np.log(maxC))

    d = (p - 1.0) * (logC[pos] - logCmax)
    d = np.clip(d, -745.0, 0.0)
    w_un = np.exp(d)
    q = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
    q[pos] = w_un
    q_sum = float(q.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(q_sum) or q_sum <= 0.0:
        q[:] = 1.0 / float(Lc)
        q_sum = 1.0
        A = float(C.sum()) / float(Lc)
        if A <= 0.0:
            A = maxC
        h = np.full(n, float(a.sum()) / Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.clip(h / A - 1.0, -20.0, 20.0)

    q /= q_sum

    logD = (p - 1.0) * logCmax + float(np.log(q_sum))

    dnum = p * (logC[pos] - logCmax)
    dnum = np.clip(dnum, -745.0, 0.0)
    sum_num = float(np.exp(dnum).sum())
    if not math.isfinite(sum_num) or sum_num <= 0.0:
        A = maxC
    else:
        logNum = p * logCmax + float(np.log(sum_num))
        A = float(np.exp(logNum - logD))
        if not math.isfinite(A) or A <= 0.0:
            A = maxC
        if A > maxC * 1.25:
            A = maxC

    # h_i = sum_k q_k a_{k-i}.
    # Use F_ar = exp(-2*pi*i*(n-1)*k/L) * conjugate(F_a).
    k = np.arange(F_a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    phase = np.exp(-2.0j * math.pi * float(n - 1) * k / float(fft_len))
    F_ar = phase * np.conjugate(F_a)
    F_q = np.fft.rfft(q, fft_len)
    h_full = np.fft.irfft(F_q * F_ar, fft_len)
    h = h_full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)

    c = h / A - 1.0
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.clip(c, -20.0, 20.0)


def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=8, scale=1.0):
    """Low-frequency smooth pseudo-Gaussian noise by a small cosine sum."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = max(1, min(int(max_freq), n // 2))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    amp = 1.0
    tot = 0.0
    for ell in range(1, m + 1):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(ell) * t + ph)
        tot += amp
        amp *= 0.6
    if tot > 0.0:
        z *= scale / tot
    return z


def add_candidate(cands, a):
    """Append a candidate if it is valid, normalized, and finite-scored."""
    b = normalize_positive(a)
    if b is None:
        return
    sc = fast_score(b)
    if math.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((sc, b))


def unique_candidates(cands, tol=1e-5):
    """Remove near-duplicates among sorted candidates."""
    out = []
    for sc, a in cands:
        dup = False
        for _, b in out:
            if a.size == b.size:
                if float(np.max(np.abs(a - b))) < tol:
                    dup = True
                    break
        if not dup:
            out.append((sc, a))
    return out


def make_flat(n):
    """The classic binomial-square-root profile (1-z)^-1/2 truncated."""
    return 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, int(n) + 1, dtype=np.float64))


def make_static_candidates(n, parent, rng, random_count=28):
    """Deterministic and random smooth starting profiles."""
    cands = []
    n = int(n)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    ar = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)

    # Base flat profile.
    add_candidate(cands, make_flat(n))

    # Simple signed linear corrections to the flat profile.
    base = make_flat(n)
    for lam in (-0.6, -0.3, -0.1, 0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        a = base * (1.0 + lam * t)
        if float(a.min()) > 0.0:
            add_candidate(cands, a)

    # Powers.
    for alpha in (-0.45, -0.30, -0.15, 0.0, 0.15, 0.30, 0.50):
        a = ar ** alpha
        add_candidate(cands, a)

    # Moderate quadratic shapes.
    for c0 in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5):
        for c1 in (-0.5, 0.0, 0.5):
            x = -0.5 * np.log1p(n * t) + c0 * t + c1 * t * t
            a = np.exp(np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0))
            add_candidate(cands, a)

    # Random smooth profiles.
    for seed in range(int(random_count)):
        r = np.random.default_rng(seed + 101)
        x = -0.5 * np.log1p(n * t)
        x += r.uniform(-0.8, 1.2) * t
        x += r.normal(0.0, 0.25)
        x += smooth_noise(n, r, max_freq=6, scale=0.35)
        a = np.exp(np.clip(x, -50.0, 50.0))
        add_candidate(cands, a)

    # Parent profile if available.
    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1:
            p = p.ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            if p.size <= 12000:
                add_candidate(cands, p)
            else:
                b = resample_to_length(p, n)
                add_candidate(cands, b)

    return cands


def resample_to_length(a, n_out):
    """Resample a positive vector on [0,1] to length n_out in log space."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    n_in = a.size
    if n_in == 0:
        return None
    if n_in == int(n_out):
        return normalize_positive(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_in)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_out))
    aa = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    loga = np.log(aa)
    logb = np.interp(x_new, x_old, loga)
    b = np.exp(np.clip(logb, -50.0, 50.0))
    return normalize_positive(b)


def optimize_sequence(a0, stop_time, seed=0, stage_len=350):
    """
    Lp-annealed multiplicative refinement.  Returns (best_seq, best_score).
    """
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = a.size
    if n < 16:
        return a, fast_score(a)
    if n > 12000:
        return None, float("inf")

    L = opt_fft_len(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    p_schedule = (2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0, 8.0, 12.0, 16.0, 24.0, 32.0)

    a = a.copy()
    _, C0 = get_fft_conv(a, L)
    cur_score = score_from_conv(a, C0)
    if not math.isfinite(cur_score):
        return None, float("inf")

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    eta = 0.12
    accepted = 0
    stage_accepted = 0
    cur_stage = -1
    cur_J = None
    stuck = 0

    start_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        F_a, C = get_fft_conv(a, L)
        score = score_from_conv(a, C)
        if not math.isfinite(score):
            break

        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            stuck += 1

        if time.monotonic() - start_print > 60.0:
            print(f"  opt n={n}: cur={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} "
                  f"eta={eta:.3f} stuck={stuck}", flush=True)
            start_print = time.monotonic()

        # Hard recovery if we drifted badly or are stuck.
        if stuck > 5000 or (not math.isfinite(score) and best_score < float("inf")):
            a = best.copy()
            _, C = get_fft_conv(a, L)
            score = score_from_conv(a, C)
            noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=5, scale=0.12)
            a = a * np.exp(noise)
            a = normalize_positive(a)
            if a is None:
                break
            cur_score = fast_score(a)
            eta = 0.12
            stage_accepted = 0
            cur_stage = max(0, cur_stage - 1)
            cur_J = None
            stuck = 0
            continue

        stage = max(0, accepted // stage_len)
        p = p_schedule[min(stage, len(p_schedule) - 1)]

        if stage != cur_stage:
            cur_J = lp_norm_from_C(C, p)
            cur_stage = stage
            stage_accepted = 0

        c = lpx_direction(a, C, p, F_a, L)
        if not math.isfinite(c).any():
            stuck += 1
            eta *= 0.7
            continue

        accepted_this = False
        for try_eta in (eta, 0.5 * eta):
            if try_eta < 1e-5:
                continue
            new_a = a * np.exp(-try_eta * c)
            new_a = np.where(np.isfinite(new_a), new_a, 0.0)
            new_a = np.maximum(new_a, 1e-300)
            snew = float(new_a.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 1e-12:
                continue
            new_a /= snew

            _, Cnew = get_fft_conv(new_a, L)
            new_score = score_from_conv(new_a, Cnew)
            if not math.isfinite(new_score):
                continue

            accept = False
            if new_score < cur_score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif cur_score < 2.5 and cur_J is not None:
                new_J = lp_norm_from_C(Cnew, p)
                allow = max(2.50, 1.25 * cur_score)
                if new_J < cur_J - 1e-12 and new_score < allow:
                    accept = True

            if accept:
                a = new_a
                cur_score = new_score
                eta = min(0.45, try_eta * 1.12)
                accepted += 1
                stage_accepted += 1
                accepted_this = True
                break

        if not accepted_this:
            stuck += 5
            eta *= 0.75
            if eta < 1e-4:
                # mild smooth noise to escape, recenter to best state
                noise = smooth_noise(n, rng, max_freq=4, scale=0.08)
                a = a * np.exp(noise)
                a = normalize_positive(a)
                if a is None:
                    break
                cur_score = fast_score(a)
                eta = 0.15
                cur_J = None
                stuck = 0

    return best, best_score


def lp_improve(a_in, stop_time, max_iter=8):
    """
    One or more SOTA-style LP refinement steps.
    Solves max sum(g) s.t. g>=0 and convolve(f,g)<=max(convolve(f,f)).
    Returns (best, score, improved).
    """
    f = normalize_positive(a_in)
    if f is None:
        return None, float("inf"), False

    n = f.size
    if n < 64 or n > 512:
        return f, fast_score(f), False

    # Keep LP coefficients safely positive and at reasonable scale.
    f = f.copy()
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-8 * float(f.mean()))
    f /= float(f.sum())

    cur_score = fast_score(f)
    best = f.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    improved = False

    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    blen = 2 * n - 1
    offsets = list(range(n))

    for it in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
            break

        _, C = get_fft_conv(f)
        M = float(C.max())
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        # Build convolution operator A with (A g)_k = sum_j f_{k-j} g_j.
        data = []
        for m in range(n):
            data.append(np.full(n, float(f[m]), dtype=np.float64))
        try:
            A = scipy.sparse.diags(data, offsets=offsets, shape=(blen, n),
                                   format="csc")
            res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
                -ones,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=M * np.ones(blen, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("  LP failed:", exc, flush=True)
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        sg = float(g.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-12:
            break

        g_scaled = g * float(f.sum()) / sg
        step_ok = False
        for tval in (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.15, 0.05):
            fn = (1.0 - tval) * f + tval * g_scaled
            fn = np.maximum(fn, 1e-12)
            fn = normalize_positive(fn)
            if fn is None:
                continue
            sc = fast_score(fn)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                f = fn
                cur_score = sc
                improved = True
                step_ok = True
                break

        if not step_ok:
            break

        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best = f.copy()

        print(f"  LP n={n} iter={it}: score={cur_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    return best, best_score, improved


def block_perturb_search(a0, stop_time, seed=0):
    """Short multiplicative block perturbations around the current best."""
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = a.size
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
        width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 800), max(2, n // 24)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        cand = best.copy()
        logmul = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.16))
        cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(logmul))

        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])

        cand = normalize_positive(cand)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = fast_score(cand)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Generate the best nonnegative coefficient sequence found within budget_s.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0

    # Leave room for final exact evaluation and return.
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(10.0, budget - 75.0)

    # Determine main search length.
    N = 6121
    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    try:
        if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
            plen = len(parent)
            if 2048 <= plen <= 8192:
                N = int(plen)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"Starting propose_candidate: seed={seed}, budget={budget:.1f}s, N={N}",
          flush=True)
    print(f"Target: {TARGET}", flush=True)

    # 1. Build and rank starting candidates.
    cands = make_static_candidates(N, parent, rng, random_count=36)
    cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    cands = unique_candidates(cands, tol=1e-5)

    # Guarantee at least the classic finite flat profile.
    flat_N = normalize_positive(make_flat(N))
    if flat_N is None:
        flat_N = np.ones(N, dtype=np.float64)
        flat_N /= float(flat_N.sum())
    if len(cands) == 0 or cands[0][0] > fast_score(flat_N):
        cands.insert(0, (fast_score(flat_N), flat_N))

    # Keep only worthwhile starting points.
    cands = cands[:16]
    for i, (sc, a) in enumerate(cands[:8]):
        print(f"  init[{i}] n={a.size} score={sc:.6f}", flush=True)

    global_best = cands[0][1].copy()
    global_score = float(cands[0][0])
    if not math.isfinite(global_score):
        global_best = flat_N.copy()
        global_score = fast_score(global_best)

    print(f"  initial global: n={global_best.size} score={global_score:.6f}",
          flush=True)

    # 2. Main Lp multiplicative optimization from top profiles.
    used_candidate_sigs = set()
    max_candidates_to_opt = 8
    idx = 0
    while idx < min(max_candidates_to_opt, len(cands)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining = deadline - now
        if remaining < 90.0:
            break

        sc0, a0 = cands[idx]
        sig = (round(sc0, 6), a0.size)
        if sig in used_candidate_sigs:
            idx += 1
            continue
        used_candidate_sigs.add(sig)

        if idx < 3:
            alloc = min(remaining * 0.42, 780.0)
        elif idx < 5:
            alloc = min(remaining * 0.24, 360.0)
        else:
            alloc = min(remaining * 0.15, 210.0)

        if alloc < 45.0:
            break

        stop = now + alloc
        print(f"Optimizing candidate idx={idx}, n={a0.size}, "
              f"init={sc0:.6f}, alloc={alloc:.0f}s", flush=True)
        try:
            b, bs = optimize_sequence(
                a0.copy(),
                stop=stop,
                seed=seed + 1000 + 17 * idx
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("  optimizer exception:", exc, flush=True)
            b, bs = None, float("inf")

        if b is not None and math.isfinite(bs) and bs < global_score:
            global_best = b.copy()
            global_score = float(bs)
            print(f"  new global from idx={idx}: n={global_best.size} "
                  f"score={global_score:.6f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"  idx={idx} no improvement; local best={bs:.6f}", flush=True)

        # Occasionally allow a short secondary ride on the returned shape
        # if it is much better than the initial candidate.
        if (b is not None and math.isfinite(bs) and bs < sc0 - 1e-5):
            rem2 = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem2 > 120.0:
                stop2 = time.monotonic() + min(rem2 * 0.18, 160.0)
                b2, bs2 = optimize_sequence(
                    b.copy(),
                    stop=stop2,
                    seed=seed + 5000 + 31 * idx
                )
                if b2 is not None and math.isfinite(bs2) and bs2 < global_score:
                    global_best = b2.copy()
                    global_score = float(bs2)
                    print(f"  secondary improvement: n={global_best.size} "
                          f"score={global_score:.6f}", flush=True)

        idx += 1

    # 3. Small LP/SOTA refinement from current best or a flat seed.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = deadline - now
    if remaining > 130.0 and global_score > 1.45:
        lp_seed = global_best
        if lp_seed.size > 512:
            lp_seed = resample_to_length(lp_seed, 256)
        if lp_seed is None:
            lp_seed = normalize_positive(make_flat(256))
        lp_stop = now + min(remaining * 0.35, 240.0)
        print("Trying LP refinement ...", flush=True)
        try:
            lp_best, lp_score, lp_impr = lp_improve(lp_seed, lp_stop, max_iter=6)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("LP top-level failed:", exc, flush=True)
            lp_best, lp_score, lp_impr = None, float("inf"), False

        if lp_best is not None and math.isfinite(lp_score):
            if lp_score < global_score - 1e-7:
                global_best = lp_best.copy()
                global_score = float(lp_score)
                print(f"  LP returned direct global: n={global_best.size} "
                      f"score={global_score:.6f}", flush=True)
            if lp_impr:
                expanded = resample_to_length(lp_best, N)
                if expanded is not None:
                    rem3 = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if rem3 > 90.0:
                        stop3 = time.monotonic() + min(rem3 * 0.30, 240.0)
                        b3, bs3 = optimize_sequence(
                            expanded,
                            stop=stop3,
                            seed=seed + 9000
                        )
                        if b3 is not None and math.isfinite(bs3) and bs3 < global_score:
                            global_best = b3.copy()
                            global_score = float(bs3)
                            print(f"  LP-expanded global: n={global_best.size} "
                                  f"score={global_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # 4. Final polishing with block perturbations on global best.
    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = deadline - now
    if remaining > 45.0:
        pol_stop = now + min(remaining * 0.45, 180.0)
        print("Polishing with block perturbations ...", flush=True)
        pb, ps = block_perturb_search(
            global_best,
            stop=pol_stop,
            seed=seed + 7777
        )
        if pb is not None and math.isfinite(ps) and ps < global_score:
            global_best = pb.copy()
            global_score = float(ps)
            print(f"  polishing improvement: n={global_best.size} "
                  f"score={global_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    # If optimization somehow returned a very bad sequence, force the flat
    # base profile and possibly a little block polish.
    if not math.isfinite(global_score) or global_score > 2.0:
        global_best = flat_N.copy()
        global_score = fast_score(global_best)

    # 5. Sanitize and return a Python list.  Final exact check.
    out = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-6:
        out = flat_N.copy()
        s = float(out.sum())
    else:
        # Keep total sum exactly 1 in floating point for stable evaluation.
        out = out / s
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    # Preserve length, but ensure no accidental zero-length.
    if out.size == 0:
        out = flat_N

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out.tolist()]

    try:
        exact = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
    except Exception as exc:
        exact = float("inf")
        print("Final exact evaluation exception:", exc, flush=True)

    if (not math.isfinite(exact)) or (exact > global_score + 0.15):
        # Fall back to clean classic flat profile if anything is off.
        out = flat_N
        out_list = [float(x) for x in out.tolist()]
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence(out_list)
        except Exception:
            exact = float("inf")

    print(f"FINAL: length={len(out_list)} fast_best={global_score:.6f} "
          f"exact={exact:.6f}", flush=True)
    return out_list
```
